========== \backend\src\main\resources\db\migration\V1__ghoomi_partition1_initial_schema.sql ==========
-- Ghoomi Partition 1 reference schema (MySQL 8.4 LTS / InnoDB).
-- Generate UUIDs in Java (UUID.randomUUID().toString()); persist as CHAR(36) ASCII BINARY collation.
-- All DATETIME(6) values are UTC by application contract. Set JDBC connectionTimeZone=UTC and configure UTC session time zone.
-- Flyway should manage versioned migrations. Run only on a fresh development/staging database after review.
-- Do not disable FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS for normal application writes.
SET time_zone = '+00:00';

CREATE TABLE users (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  email VARCHAR(254) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  email_verified_at DATETIME(6),
  role VARCHAR(191) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER' CHECK (role IN ('USER','SUPPORT','ADMIN','SUPER_ADMIN')),
  is_active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE user_profiles (
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  display_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  phone_e164 VARCHAR(20),
  travel_preferences JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  avatar_object_key TEXT,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_user_profiles_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE saved_travellers (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  owner_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  display_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  age_category VARCHAR(191) CHECK (age_category IN ('CHILD','ADULT','SENIOR','UNSPECIFIED')),
  notes TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_saved_travellers_owner_user_id FOREIGN KEY (owner_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE emergency_contacts (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  owner_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  contact_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  contact_phone_e164 VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  relationship_label VARCHAR(100),
  verified_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_emergency_contacts_owner_user_id FOREIGN KEY (owner_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE destinations (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  slug VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  state_region VARCHAR(150),
  country_code CHAR(2) NOT NULL,
  latitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  longitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  summary TEXT,
  content_source TEXT,
  image_license_ref TEXT,
  active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE places (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  destination_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL CHECK (category IN ('ATTRACTION','RESTAURANT','ACTIVITY','OTHER')),
  address_label TEXT,
  latitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  longitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  description TEXT,
  source_name VARCHAR(180),
  source_url TEXT,
  info_verified_at DATETIME(6),
  opening_hours JSON,
  estimated_cost_minor BIGINT CHECK (estimated_cost_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_places_destination_id FOREIGN KEY (destination_id) REFERENCES destinations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE providers (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  code VARCHAR(80) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  vertical VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL CHECK (vertical IN ('FLIGHT','TRAIN','BUS','CAB','STAY','ACTIVITY','MAP','PAYMENT')),
  capability VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PLANNING_ONLY'
    CHECK (capability IN ('PLANNING_ONLY','REDIRECT','LIVE_SEARCH_EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT','DIRECT_BOOKING')),
  authorization_status VARCHAR(25) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNVERIFIED'
    CHECK (authorization_status IN ('UNVERIFIED','UNDER_REVIEW','APPROVED','SUSPENDED')),
  credentials_ref VARCHAR(255), -- secret-manager reference only
  terms_url TEXT,
  enabled TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE provider_offers (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  provider_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  provider_offer_key VARCHAR(160) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  vertical VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  details JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  origin_label VARCHAR(200),
  destination_label VARCHAR(200),
  starts_at DATETIME(6),
  ends_at DATETIME(6),
  price_minor BIGINT CHECK (price_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  price_basis VARCHAR(24) CHECK (price_basis IN ('PER_PERSON','PER_GROUP','PER_ROOM','TOTAL','UNKNOWN')),
  data_status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL CHECK (data_status IN ('VERIFIED_LIVE','PROVIDER_LISTED','ESTIMATED','UNAVAILABLE')),
  checked_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  valid_until DATETIME(6),
  terms_url TEXT,
  redirect_url TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(provider_id, provider_offer_key, checked_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_provider_offers_provider_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_id) REFERENCES providers(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE trips (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  owner_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  destination_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  origin_label VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  destination_label VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  start_date DATE NOT NULL,
  end_date DATE NOT NULL,
  timezone VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Asia/Kolkata',
  traveler_count INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (traveler_count > 0 AND traveler_count <= 50),
  plan_mode VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BALANCED'
    CHECK (plan_mode IN ('BUDGET','BALANCED','COMFORT','CUSTOM')),
  target_budget_minor BIGINT CHECK (target_budget_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INR',
  own_vehicle TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  already_have_stay TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  preferences JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
    CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','PLANNED','ACTIVE','COMPLETED','CANCELLED')),
  version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (version >= 1),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CHECK (end_date >= start_date),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trips_owner_user_id FOREIGN KEY (owner_user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trips_destination_id FOREIGN KEY (destination_id) REFERENCES destinations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE itinerary_days (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  day_date DATE NOT NULL,
  day_index INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (day_index >= 1),
  title VARCHAR(255),
  UNIQUE(trip_id,day_date),
  UNIQUE(trip_id,day_index),
  CONSTRAINT fk_itinerary_days_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE itinerary_items (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  itinerary_day_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  place_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  item_order INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (item_order >= 0),
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  planned_start TIME,
  planned_end TIME,
  notes TEXT,
  estimate_minor BIGINT CHECK (estimate_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  verification_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER_PLANNED'
    CHECK (verification_status IN ('USER_PLANNED','AI_SUGGESTED','PROVIDER_LISTED','VERIFIED')),
  source_ref TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(itinerary_day_id,item_order),
  CONSTRAINT fk_itinerary_items_itinerary_day_id FOREIGN KEY (itinerary_day_id) REFERENCES itinerary_days(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_itinerary_items_place_id FOREIGN KEY (place_id) REFERENCES places(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE trip_budget_items (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL CHECK (category IN ('TRANSPORT','STAY','FOOD','LOCAL_TRAVEL','ACTIVITY','FEES','EMERGENCY_RESERVE','OTHER')),
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,
  basis VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'TOTAL' CHECK (basis IN ('TOTAL','PER_PERSON','PER_NIGHT','PER_UNIT')),
  price_status VARCHAR(28) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ESTIMATED'
    CHECK (price_status IN ('ESTIMATED','PROVIDER_QUOTED','USER_ENTERED','PAID_VERIFIED')),
  source_ref TEXT,
  checked_at DATETIME(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trip_budget_items_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE trip_offer_selections (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  provider_offer_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  selection_kind VARCHAR(28) NOT NULL CHECK (selection_kind IN ('SUPPLIER_OFFER','OWN_VEHICLE','OWN_STAY','CUSTOM')),
  vertical VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  booking_state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PLANNED'
    CHECK (booking_state IN ('PLANNED','REDIRECTED_EXTERNAL','MANUAL_REFERENCE_UNVERIFIED','CONFIRMED','CANCELLED')),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trip_offer_selections_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_trip_offer_selections_provider_offer_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_offer_id) REFERENCES provider_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE ai_plan_runs (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  requested_by CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  run_scope VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL CHECK (run_scope IN ('WHOLE_TRIP','SINGLE_DAY')),
  model_ref VARCHAR(100),
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','SUCCEEDED','FAILED','VALIDATION_REJECTED')),
  request_context_hash VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  duration_ms INTEGER,
  error_code VARCHAR(80),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  completed_at DATETIME(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_ai_plan_runs_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_ai_plan_runs_requested_by FOREIGN KEY (requested_by) REFERENCES users(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE booking_intents (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  provider_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  offer_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  capability VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL CHECK (capability IN ('REDIRECT','LIVE_SEARCH_EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT','DIRECT_BOOKING')),
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT_INTENT','REDIRECTED_EXTERNAL','PENDING','PROCESSING','CONFIRMED','FAILED','UNKNOWN','CANCELLED')),
  quoted_amount_minor BIGINT CHECK (quoted_amount_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  quote_expires_at DATETIME(6),
  terms_accepted_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_provider_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_id) REFERENCES providers(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_offer_id FOREIGN KEY (offer_id) REFERENCES provider_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE bookings (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  booking_intent_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  provider_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  provider_booking_ref VARCHAR(180) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','PROCESSING','CONFIRMED','FAILED','CANCELLED','UNKNOWN','COMPLETED')),
  confirmed_at DATETIME(6),
  provider_evidence_ref TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CHECK (status <> 'CONFIRMED' OR (provider_booking_ref IS NOT NULL AND provider_evidence_ref IS NOT NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_booking_intent_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_intent_id) REFERENCES booking_intents(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_provider_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_id) REFERENCES providers(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE booking_events (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  booking_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  provider_event_ref VARCHAR(191) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  event_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  source VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  observed_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  safe_summary JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_events_booking_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_id) REFERENCES bookings(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE payment_attempts (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  booking_intent_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  gateway_code VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  gateway_payment_ref VARCHAR(180) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('NOT_STARTED','INITIATED','PENDING','SUCCEEDED','FAILED','UNKNOWN')),
  verified_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key),
  CONSTRAINT fk_payment_attempts_booking_intent_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_intent_id) REFERENCES booking_intents(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_payment_attempts_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE webhook_receipts (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  source_code VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  external_event_id VARCHAR(180) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  payload_hash VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  signature_valid TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  processing_status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RECEIVED'
    CHECK (processing_status IN ('RECEIVED','PROCESSED','FAILED','IGNORED')),
  received_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  processed_at DATETIME(6),
  UNIQUE(source_code,external_event_id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE notifications (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  body TEXT NOT NULL,
  channel VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IN_APP',
  delivery_status VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'
    CHECK (delivery_status IN ('PENDING','SENT','DELIVERED','FAILED','NOT_APPLICABLE')),
  read_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_notifications_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_notifications_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE support_tickets (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  booking_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  assigned_staff_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  subject VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  description TEXT NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN'
    CHECK (status IN ('OPEN','IN_REVIEW','WAITING_FOR_USER','RESOLVED','CLOSED')),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_booking_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_id) REFERENCES bookings(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_assigned_staff_user_id FOREIGN KEY (assigned_staff_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE safety_resources (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  country_code CHAR(2) NOT NULL,
  region_label VARCHAR(191),
  resource_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  display_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  phone_number VARCHAR(25),
  official_url TEXT,
  source_name VARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
  verified_at DATETIME(6),
  active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE user_consents (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  consent_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  document_version VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  purpose VARCHAR(200),
  granted_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  withdrawn_at DATETIME(6),
  client_context JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  CONSTRAINT fk_user_consents_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  actor_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  action VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  object_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  object_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  request_id VARCHAR(120) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  safe_metadata JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_audit_logs_actor_user_id FOREIGN KEY (actor_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE INDEX saved_travellers_owner_idx ON saved_travellers(owner_user_id);

CREATE INDEX emergency_contacts_owner_idx ON emergency_contacts(owner_user_id);

CREATE INDEX places_destination_cat_idx ON places(destination_id,category);

CREATE INDEX provider_offers_provider_checked_idx ON provider_offers(provider_id,checked_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX trips_owner_updated_idx ON trips(owner_user_id,updated_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX trips_status_start_idx ON trips(status,start_date);

CREATE INDEX itinerary_items_day_idx ON itinerary_items(itinerary_day_id,item_order);

CREATE INDEX trip_budget_items_trip_idx ON trip_budget_items(trip_id);

CREATE INDEX trip_offer_selections_trip_idx ON trip_offer_selections(trip_id);

CREATE INDEX ai_plan_runs_trip_created_idx ON ai_plan_runs(trip_id,created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX booking_intents_trip_idx ON booking_intents(trip_id);

-- UNIQUE indexes in MySQL permit multiple NULL values; no partial WHERE required.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bookings_supplier_reference_uq ON bookings(provider_id,provider_booking_ref);

CREATE INDEX bookings_user_created_idx ON bookings(user_id,created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX booking_events_booking_idx ON booking_events(booking_id,observed_at DESC);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payment_gateway_ref_uq ON payment_attempts(gateway_code,gateway_payment_ref);

CREATE INDEX notifications_user_created_idx ON notifications(user_id,created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX support_tickets_owner_status_idx ON support_tickets(user_id,status);

CREATE INDEX user_consents_type_idx ON user_consents(user_id,consent_type,granted_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_logs_object_idx ON audit_logs(object_type,object_id,created_at DESC);



========== \backend\src\main\resources\db\migration\V2__add_otp_records.sql ==========
-- ============================================================
-- V2: Add otp_records table for password-reset OTP flow
-- Author : Backend Team
-- Applied: Partition 1 / IMP-02
-- Rules  : Flyway migrations are APPEND-ONLY. Never alter applied files.
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS otp_records (
    id          CHAR(36)     NOT NULL           COMMENT 'Application-generated UUID',
    email       VARCHAR(254) NOT NULL           COMMENT 'Target email address',
    otp_hash    VARCHAR(72)  NOT NULL           COMMENT 'BCrypt hash of the 6-digit OTP',
    purpose     ENUM('PASSWORD_RESET') NOT NULL COMMENT 'OTP use-case discriminator',
    expires_at  DATETIME(6)  NOT NULL           COMMENT 'UTC expiry timestamp',
    used        TINYINT(1)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 COMMENT 'Consumed flag (single-use)',
    attempts    TINYINT      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 COMMENT 'Failed verification count',
    created_at  DATETIME(6)  NOT NULL           COMMENT 'UTC creation timestamp',

    PRIMARY KEY (id),
    INDEX idx_otp_email_purpose (email, purpose),
    INDEX idx_otp_expires_at    (expires_at)
) ENGINE=InnoDB
  DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4
  COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci
  COMMENT='Single-use OTP records for password reset';



========== \backend\src\test\java\com\ghoomi\modules\health\HealthControllerTest.java ==========
package com.ghoomi.modules.health;

import com.ghoomi.common.config.SecurityConfig;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.web.servlet.WebMvcTest;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

@WebMvcTest(HealthController.class)
@Import(SecurityConfig.class)
class HealthControllerTest {

    @Autowired
    private MockMvc mockMvc;

    @Test
    void healthCheckShouldReturnOk() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/api/v1/health"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.success").value(true))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.message").value("Ghoomi backend is operational"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.status").value("UP"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.service").value("ghoomi-backend"));
    }
}



========== \database\mysql\V1__ghoomi_partition1_reference.sql ==========
-- Ghoomi Partition 1 reference schema (MySQL 8.4 LTS / InnoDB).
-- Generate UUIDs in Java (UUID.randomUUID().toString()); persist as CHAR(36) ASCII BINARY collation.
-- All DATETIME(6) values are UTC by application contract. Set JDBC connectionTimeZone=UTC and configure UTC session time zone.
-- Flyway should manage versioned migrations. Run only on a fresh development/staging database after review.
-- Do not disable FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS for normal application writes.
SET time_zone = '+00:00';

CREATE TABLE users (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  email VARCHAR(254) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  email_verified_at DATETIME(6),
  role VARCHAR(191) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER' CHECK (role IN ('USER','SUPPORT','ADMIN','SUPER_ADMIN')),
  is_active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE user_profiles (
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  display_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  phone_e164 VARCHAR(20),
  travel_preferences JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  avatar_object_key TEXT,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_user_profiles_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE saved_travellers (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  owner_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  display_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  age_category VARCHAR(191) CHECK (age_category IN ('CHILD','ADULT','SENIOR','UNSPECIFIED')),
  notes TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_saved_travellers_owner_user_id FOREIGN KEY (owner_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE emergency_contacts (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  owner_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  contact_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  contact_phone_e164 VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  relationship_label VARCHAR(100),
  verified_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_emergency_contacts_owner_user_id FOREIGN KEY (owner_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE destinations (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  slug VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  state_region VARCHAR(150),
  country_code CHAR(2) NOT NULL,
  latitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  longitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  summary TEXT,
  content_source TEXT,
  image_license_ref TEXT,
  active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE places (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  destination_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL CHECK (category IN ('ATTRACTION','RESTAURANT','ACTIVITY','OTHER')),
  address_label TEXT,
  latitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  longitude DECIMAL(9,6),
  description TEXT,
  source_name VARCHAR(180),
  source_url TEXT,
  info_verified_at DATETIME(6),
  opening_hours JSON,
  estimated_cost_minor BIGINT CHECK (estimated_cost_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_places_destination_id FOREIGN KEY (destination_id) REFERENCES destinations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE providers (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  code VARCHAR(80) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  vertical VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL CHECK (vertical IN ('FLIGHT','TRAIN','BUS','CAB','STAY','ACTIVITY','MAP','PAYMENT')),
  capability VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PLANNING_ONLY'
    CHECK (capability IN ('PLANNING_ONLY','REDIRECT','LIVE_SEARCH_EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT','DIRECT_BOOKING')),
  authorization_status VARCHAR(25) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNVERIFIED'
    CHECK (authorization_status IN ('UNVERIFIED','UNDER_REVIEW','APPROVED','SUSPENDED')),
  credentials_ref VARCHAR(255), -- secret-manager reference only
  terms_url TEXT,
  enabled TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE provider_offers (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  provider_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  provider_offer_key VARCHAR(160) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  vertical VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  details JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  origin_label VARCHAR(200),
  destination_label VARCHAR(200),
  starts_at DATETIME(6),
  ends_at DATETIME(6),
  price_minor BIGINT CHECK (price_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  price_basis VARCHAR(24) CHECK (price_basis IN ('PER_PERSON','PER_GROUP','PER_ROOM','TOTAL','UNKNOWN')),
  data_status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL CHECK (data_status IN ('VERIFIED_LIVE','PROVIDER_LISTED','ESTIMATED','UNAVAILABLE')),
  checked_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
  valid_until DATETIME(6),
  terms_url TEXT,
  redirect_url TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(provider_id, provider_offer_key, checked_at),
  CONSTRAINT fk_provider_offers_provider_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_id) REFERENCES providers(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE trips (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  owner_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  destination_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  origin_label VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  destination_label VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  start_date DATE NOT NULL,
  end_date DATE NOT NULL,
  timezone VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Asia/Kolkata',
  traveler_count INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (traveler_count > 0 AND traveler_count <= 50),
  plan_mode VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BALANCED'
    CHECK (plan_mode IN ('BUDGET','BALANCED','COMFORT','CUSTOM')),
  target_budget_minor BIGINT CHECK (target_budget_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INR',
  own_vehicle TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  already_have_stay TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  preferences JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
    CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','PLANNED','ACTIVE','COMPLETED','CANCELLED')),
  version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (version >= 1),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CHECK (end_date >= start_date),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trips_owner_user_id FOREIGN KEY (owner_user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trips_destination_id FOREIGN KEY (destination_id) REFERENCES destinations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE itinerary_days (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  day_date DATE NOT NULL,
  day_index INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (day_index >= 1),
  title VARCHAR(255),
  UNIQUE(trip_id,day_date),
  UNIQUE(trip_id,day_index),
  CONSTRAINT fk_itinerary_days_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE itinerary_items (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  itinerary_day_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  place_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  item_order INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (item_order >= 0),
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  planned_start TIME,
  planned_end TIME,
  notes TEXT,
  estimate_minor BIGINT CHECK (estimate_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  verification_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USER_PLANNED'
    CHECK (verification_status IN ('USER_PLANNED','AI_SUGGESTED','PROVIDER_LISTED','VERIFIED')),
  source_ref TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(itinerary_day_id,item_order),
  CONSTRAINT fk_itinerary_items_itinerary_day_id FOREIGN KEY (itinerary_day_id) REFERENCES itinerary_days(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_itinerary_items_place_id FOREIGN KEY (place_id) REFERENCES places(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE trip_budget_items (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL CHECK (category IN ('TRANSPORT','STAY','FOOD','LOCAL_TRAVEL','ACTIVITY','FEES','EMERGENCY_RESERVE','OTHER')),
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,
  basis VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'TOTAL' CHECK (basis IN ('TOTAL','PER_PERSON','PER_NIGHT','PER_UNIT')),
  price_status VARCHAR(28) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ESTIMATED'
    CHECK (price_status IN ('ESTIMATED','PROVIDER_QUOTED','USER_ENTERED','PAID_VERIFIED')),
  source_ref TEXT,
  checked_at DATETIME(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trip_budget_items_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE trip_offer_selections (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  provider_offer_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  selection_kind VARCHAR(28) NOT NULL CHECK (selection_kind IN ('SUPPLIER_OFFER','OWN_VEHICLE','OWN_STAY','CUSTOM')),
  vertical VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  booking_state VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PLANNED'
    CHECK (booking_state IN ('PLANNED','REDIRECTED_EXTERNAL','MANUAL_REFERENCE_UNVERIFIED','CONFIRMED','CANCELLED')),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_trip_offer_selections_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_trip_offer_selections_provider_offer_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_offer_id) REFERENCES provider_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE ai_plan_runs (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  requested_by CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  run_scope VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL CHECK (run_scope IN ('WHOLE_TRIP','SINGLE_DAY')),
  model_ref VARCHAR(100),
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','SUCCEEDED','FAILED','VALIDATION_REJECTED')),
  request_context_hash VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  duration_ms INTEGER,
  error_code VARCHAR(80),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  completed_at DATETIME(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_ai_plan_runs_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_ai_plan_runs_requested_by FOREIGN KEY (requested_by) REFERENCES users(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE booking_intents (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  provider_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  offer_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  capability VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL CHECK (capability IN ('REDIRECT','LIVE_SEARCH_EXTERNAL_CHECKOUT','DIRECT_BOOKING')),
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT_INTENT','REDIRECTED_EXTERNAL','PENDING','PROCESSING','CONFIRMED','FAILED','UNKNOWN','CANCELLED')),
  quoted_amount_minor BIGINT CHECK (quoted_amount_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3),
  quote_expires_at DATETIME(6),
  terms_accepted_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_provider_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_id) REFERENCES providers(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_intents_offer_id FOREIGN KEY (offer_id) REFERENCES provider_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE bookings (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  booking_intent_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  provider_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  provider_booking_ref VARCHAR(180) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','PROCESSING','CONFIRMED','FAILED','CANCELLED','UNKNOWN','COMPLETED')),
  confirmed_at DATETIME(6),
  provider_evidence_ref TEXT,
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CHECK (status <> 'CONFIRMED' OR (provider_booking_ref IS NOT NULL AND provider_evidence_ref IS NOT NULL)),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_booking_intent_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_intent_id) REFERENCES booking_intents(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_provider_id FOREIGN KEY (provider_id) REFERENCES providers(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_bookings_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE booking_events (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  booking_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  provider_event_ref VARCHAR(191) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  event_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  source VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  observed_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  safe_summary JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  CONSTRAINT fk_booking_events_booking_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_id) REFERENCES bookings(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE payment_attempts (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  booking_intent_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  gateway_code VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  gateway_payment_ref VARCHAR(180) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor >= 0),
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('NOT_STARTED','INITIATED','PENDING','SUCCEEDED','FAILED','UNKNOWN')),
  verified_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  UNIQUE(user_id,idempotency_key),
  CONSTRAINT fk_payment_attempts_booking_intent_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_intent_id) REFERENCES booking_intents(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_payment_attempts_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE webhook_receipts (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  source_code VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  external_event_id VARCHAR(180) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  payload_hash VARCHAR(128) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin NOT NULL,
  signature_valid TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
  processing_status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RECEIVED'
    CHECK (processing_status IN ('RECEIVED','PROCESSED','FAILED','IGNORED')),
  received_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  processed_at DATETIME(6),
  UNIQUE(source_code,external_event_id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE notifications (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
  body TEXT NOT NULL,
  channel VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IN_APP',
  delivery_status VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'
    CHECK (delivery_status IN ('PENDING','SENT','DELIVERED','FAILED','NOT_APPLICABLE')),
  read_at DATETIME(6),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_notifications_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT fk_notifications_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE support_tickets (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  trip_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  booking_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  assigned_staff_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  category VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
  subject VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  description TEXT NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(35) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN'
    CHECK (status IN ('OPEN','IN_REVIEW','WAITING_FOR_USER','RESOLVED','CLOSED')),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  updated_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id),
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_trip_id FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES trips(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_booking_id FOREIGN KEY (booking_id) REFERENCES bookings(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT fk_support_tickets_assigned_staff_user_id FOREIGN KEY (assigned_staff_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE safety_resources (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  country_code CHAR(2) NOT NULL,
  region_label VARCHAR(191),
  resource_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  display_name VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
  phone_number VARCHAR(25),
  official_url TEXT,
  source_name VARCHAR(180) NOT NULL,
  verified_at DATETIME(6),
  active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE user_consents (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin NOT NULL,
  consent_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  document_version VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  purpose VARCHAR(200),
  granted_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  withdrawn_at DATETIME(6),
  client_context JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  CONSTRAINT fk_user_consents_user_id FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
  id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin PRIMARY KEY,
  actor_user_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  action VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  object_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
  object_id CHAR(36) CHARACTER SET ascii COLLATE ascii_bin,
  request_id VARCHAR(120) COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_bin,
  safe_metadata JSON NOT NULL DEFAULT (JSON_OBJECT()),
  created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
  CONSTRAINT fk_audit_logs_actor_user_id FOREIGN KEY (actor_user_id) REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;

CREATE INDEX saved_travellers_owner_idx ON saved_travellers(owner_user_id);

CREATE INDEX emergency_contacts_owner_idx ON emergency_contacts(owner_user_id);

CREATE INDEX places_destination_cat_idx ON places(destination_id,category);

CREATE INDEX provider_offers_provider_checked_idx ON provider_offers(provider_id,checked_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX trips_owner_updated_idx ON trips(owner_user_id,updated_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX trips_status_start_idx ON trips(status,start_date);

CREATE INDEX itinerary_items_day_idx ON itinerary_items(itinerary_day_id,item_order);

CREATE INDEX trip_budget_items_trip_idx ON trip_budget_items(trip_id);

CREATE INDEX trip_offer_selections_trip_idx ON trip_offer_selections(trip_id);

CREATE INDEX ai_plan_runs_trip_created_idx ON ai_plan_runs(trip_id,created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX booking_intents_trip_idx ON booking_intents(trip_id);

-- UNIQUE indexes in MySQL permit multiple NULL values; no partial WHERE required.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bookings_supplier_reference_uq ON bookings(provider_id,provider_booking_ref);

CREATE INDEX bookings_user_created_idx ON bookings(user_id,created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX booking_events_booking_idx ON booking_events(booking_id,observed_at DESC);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX payment_gateway_ref_uq ON payment_attempts(gateway_code,gateway_payment_ref);

CREATE INDEX notifications_user_created_idx ON notifications(user_id,created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX support_tickets_owner_status_idx ON support_tickets(user_id,status);

CREATE INDEX user_consents_type_idx ON user_consents(user_id,consent_type,granted_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX audit_logs_object_idx ON audit_logs(object_type,object_id,created_at DESC);

========== \frontend\README.md ==========
# GHOOMI — Authentication Frontend

This is production-oriented frontend code adapted from the uploaded `ghoomi-authentication-design.zip`, with the approved GHOOMI logo.
No fake accounts, hardcoded passwords, mock tokens, or success simulations are included.

## Install & run

From the repository root:

```powershell
cd frontend
npm install
npm run dev
```

Visit `http://localhost:3000/login` or `/signup`. The other pages are `/forgot-password`, `/verify-otp`, and `/reset-password`.

## Using with an existing frontend folder

Copy the contents of the `frontend` folder from this package into your project's existing `frontend` folder, preserving relative paths.
For conflicting files, back up your originals first. This bundle includes `src/app/globals.css`, `package.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `postcss.config.mjs`, and `next.config.mjs`. If your existing app has important customizations, merge those instead of overwriting them blindly.
This bundle does not touch your root documentation, SQL schema, or backend.

## Why authentication says not configured

The real backend hasn't been implemented. This app intentionally refuses to claim that login, signup, Google OAuth, or password reset succeeded without a verified API response. To connect your real backend, set `NEXT_PUBLIC_AUTH_API_BASE_URL` in `frontend/.env.local` and restart the dev server.

## API contract that backend must implement

All POST requests send JSON and use `credentials: 'include'`. On success return an HTTP 2xx JSON response with `{"success":true}`. On failures use a non-2xx response with a safe, user-facing `{"message":"..."}`. Configure allowed CORS origins and credentialed cookies correctly, as appropriate. Protect state-changing requests against CSRF.

| POST path | JSON request | Backend requirement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `/auth/login` | `{identifier,password,rememberMe}` | Verify credentials; set secure HttpOnly session cookie |
| `/auth/register` | `{name,email,password,accountType,businessCategories?}` | Create real account; `partner_application` must never grant admin role |
| `/auth/password/forgot` | `{email}` | Safely send email OTP, generic public response, rate limits; may return `retryAfterSeconds` |
| `/auth/password/verify-otp` | `{email,otp}` | Verify OTP server-side; issue short-lived HttpOnly reset authorization cookie |
| `/auth/password/reset` | `{newPassword}` | Require verified reset cookie; update password, invalidate OTP/grant/sessions as needed |
| `/auth/oauth/google/start` | `{intent:'sign_in'}` | Validate requested flow, return `{success:true,authorizationUrl:'https://...'}` for real OAuth |

The actual backend might use different routes or different response shapes. If so, adapt ONLY `src/services/auth-api.ts`, and do not fabricate responses to make integration appear functional.

## Security details

The browser temporarily stores **only the email address** in `sessionStorage` between password recovery pages. It does **not** store a password, OTP, reset token, login token, or privileged role. All OTP verification and reset authorization must occur server-side. The Admin/Franchisee selection is a public **partner application** request and cannot grant privileges.

## Assets

- `public/brand/ghoomi-logo-original.png` — exact selected logo as supplied.
- `public/brand/ghoomi-logo-navy.png` — cropped transparent navy version.
- `public/brand/ghoomi-logo-white.png` — white version for scenic/dark panels.
- `public/images/` — original scenic images from your UI ZIP.

## Testing note

The source has been prepared for Next.js + TypeScript, but dependencies could not be installed in the creation environment due to unavailable network access. Please run `npm install`, `npm run typecheck` and `npm run build` on your machine. If your existing Next.js scaffold has more recent compatible package versions, you can keep those instead of downgrading to the ZIP's versions.

========== \frontend\src\app\(authentication)\forgot-password\page.tsx ==========
'use client'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { RecoveryShell } from '@/components/auth/recovery-shell'
import { RecoveryInput, RecoveryButton, RecoveryFeedback } from '@/components/auth/recovery-controls'
import { authApi } from '@/services/auth-api'
import { emailError } from '@/components/auth/validation'
export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const submit = async (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setError(null)
    const invalid = emailError(email)
    if (invalid) { setError(invalid); return }
    setPending(true)
    try {
      await authApi.forgotPassword(email.trim())
      // Only email (not OTP/token/password) is kept in browser tab session storage.
      sessionStorage.setItem('ghoomi-reset-email', email.trim())
      router.push('/verify-otp')
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to request OTP.') }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <RecoveryShell title="Forgot your password?" subtitle="Enter your email to request a one-time verification code. If your account is eligible, the server will arrange delivery.">
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5"><RecoveryInput label="Email address" type="email" autoComplete="email" required value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="Enter your email address" /><RecoveryFeedback error={error} /><RecoveryButton loading={pending}>Send OTP</RecoveryButton></form>
  </RecoveryShell>
}

========== \frontend\src\app\(authentication)\reset-password\page.tsx ==========
'use client'
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { RecoveryShell } from '@/components/auth/recovery-shell'
import { RecoveryInput, RecoveryButton, RecoveryFeedback } from '@/components/auth/recovery-controls'
import { passwordError } from '@/components/auth/validation'
import { authApi } from '@/services/auth-api'
export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('')
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const submit = async (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setError(null)
    const invalid = passwordError(password)
    if (invalid) { setError(invalid); return }
    if (password !== confirm) { setError('Passwords do not match.'); return }
    setPending(true)
    try {
      // This endpoint must reject requests lacking the server-issued verification grant.
      await authApi.resetPassword(password)
      sessionStorage.removeItem('ghoomi-reset-email')
      setDone(true)
      setPassword(''); setConfirm('')
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to reset password.') }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <RecoveryShell title={done ? 'Password updated' : 'Create new password'} subtitle={done ? 'Your password was reset successfully by the authentication server.' : 'Choose a strong new password. A verified, unexpired reset authorization is required.'}>
    {done ? <Link href="/login" className="flex h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary font-semibold text-white">Return to Login</Link> :
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
        <RecoveryInput label="New password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <RecoveryInput label="Confirm new password" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required value={confirm} onChange={e => setConfirm(e.target.value)} />
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">At least 8 characters, uppercase, lowercase, number and symbol.</p>
        <RecoveryFeedback error={error} />
        <RecoveryButton loading={pending}>Change Password</RecoveryButton>
      </form>}
  </RecoveryShell>
}

========== \frontend\src\app\(authentication)\verify-otp\page.tsx ==========
'use client'
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { RecoveryShell } from '@/components/auth/recovery-shell'
import { RecoveryInput, RecoveryButton, RecoveryFeedback } from '@/components/auth/recovery-controls'
import { authApi } from '@/services/auth-api'
export default function VerifyOtpPage() {
  const router = useRouter()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const [resendUntil, setResendUntil] = useState(0)
  const [secondsLeft, setSecondsLeft] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => { setEmail(sessionStorage.getItem('ghoomi-reset-email') ?? '') }, [])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!resendUntil) return
    const refresh = () => setSecondsLeft(Math.max(0, Math.ceil((resendUntil - Date.now()) / 1000)))
    refresh(); const id = window.setInterval(refresh, 1000); return () => window.clearInterval(id)
  }, [resendUntil])
  const submit = async (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault(); setError(null); setMessage(null)
    if (!email) { setError('Start with the Forgot Password form to request a code.'); return }
    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(otp)) { setError('Enter the six-digit OTP.'); return }
    setPending(true)
    try {
      // Backend verifies OTP and MUST issue a short-lived HttpOnly reset grant cookie.
      await authApi.verifyOtp(email, otp)
      router.push('/reset-password')
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'OTP verification failed.') }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  const resend = async () => {
    if (pending || secondsLeft > 0 || !email) return
    setError(null); setMessage(null); setPending(true)
    try {
      const result = await authApi.forgotPassword(email)
      const sec = Number.isFinite(result.retryAfterSeconds) ? Math.max(0, Number(result.retryAfterSeconds)) : 60
      setResendUntil(Date.now() + sec * 1000)
      setMessage('The server accepted your OTP resend request. Check your email if it is eligible.')
    } catch (err) { setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to resend OTP.') }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  return <RecoveryShell title="Verify your email" subtitle="Enter the six-digit code from your email. The code is validated by the server, not this page.">
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{email ? `Verification requested for ${email}` : 'No password reset request found in this browser tab.'}</p>
      <RecoveryInput label="Six-digit OTP" type="text" inputMode="numeric" autoComplete="one-time-code" maxLength={6} pattern="[0-9]{6}" required value={otp} onChange={e => setOtp(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0,6))} placeholder="Enter code" />
      <RecoveryFeedback error={error} message={message} />
      <RecoveryButton loading={pending}>Verify OTP</RecoveryButton>
    </form>
    <button type="button" disabled={!email || pending || secondsLeft > 0} onClick={resend} className="mt-5 w-full text-center text-sm font-semibold text-primary disabled:text-muted-foreground">
      {secondsLeft > 0 ? `Resend available in ${secondsLeft}s` : 'Resend OTP'}
    </button>
  </RecoveryShell>
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\auth-card.tsx ==========

'use client'

import {
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from 'react'

import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

import { BrandPanel, MobileBrandHeader } from './brand-panel'
import { LoginForm } from './login-form'
import { SignupForm } from './signup-form'
import { FormShell } from './form-parts'

export type AuthMode = 'login' | 'signup'

const RECOVERY_ROUTES = [
  '/forgot-password',
  '/verify-otp',
  '/reset-password',
]

export function AuthCard({
  children,
}: {
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const pathname = usePathname()

  const isRecovery = RECOVERY_ROUTES.includes(pathname)
  const isSignup = pathname === '/signup'

  // Recovery uses the same side as Signup.
  const mode: AuthMode =
    isSignup || isRecovery ? 'signup' : 'login'

  const [hasSwitched, setHasSwitched] = useState(false)
  const previousPath = useRef(pathname)

  // Detect navigation, including Forgot Password links.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (previousPath.current !== pathname) {
      previousPath.current = pathname
      setHasSwitched(true)
    }
  }, [pathname])

  const switchTo = (next: AuthMode) => {
    const destination =
      next === 'login' ? '/login' : '/signup'

    if (pathname === destination) return

    setHasSwitched(true)
    router.push(destination)
  }

  return (
    <section
      aria-label={
        isRecovery
          ? 'Password Recovery'
          : isSignup
            ? 'Create Account'
            : 'Login'
      }
      className={cn(
        'ghoomi-auth-card relative w-full max-w-[1040px] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgb(23_32_51/0.04),0_30px_70px_-30px_rgb(23_32_51/0.35)]',
        !hasSwitched && 'auth-intro'
      )}
    >
      {/* Existing animated mountain panel */}
      <BrandPanel
      mode={mode}
      animate={hasSwitched}
      recovery={isRecovery}
/>
      <MobileBrandHeader recovery={isRecovery} mode={mode} />
      <div className="ghoomi-auth-grid relative z-10 grid md:h-full md:min-h-0 md:grid-cols-2 md:grid-rows-[minmax(0,1fr)]">

        {/* Original Login Form */}
        <LoginForm
          active={!isSignup && !isRecovery}
          onSwitch={() => switchTo('signup')}
          className="col-start-1 row-start-1 md:col-start-2"
        />

        {/* Original Signup Form */}
        {!isRecovery && (
          <SignupForm
            active={isSignup}
            onSwitch={() => switchTo('login')}
            className="col-start-1 row-start-1"
          />
        )}

        {/* Recovery forms inside same sliding card */}
        {isRecovery && (
          <FormShell
            active={true}
            className="ghoomi-auth-form col-start-1 row-start-1"
          >
            <div
              key={pathname}
              className="recovery-enter"
            >
              {children}
            </div>
          </FormShell>
        )}

      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\auth-field.tsx ==========
'use client'

import { Check, CircleAlert, Eye, EyeOff, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { useId, useState, type InputHTMLAttributes } from 'react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

type AuthFieldProps = Omit<InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, 'placeholder'> & {
  label: string
  icon: LucideIcon
  error?: string
  valid?: boolean
  invalid?: boolean
}

export function FieldMessage({ id, message }: { id: string; message?: string }) {
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(message)
  if (message && message !== shown) setShown(message)

  return (
    <div className="field-message" data-show={Boolean(message)} aria-hidden={!message}>
      <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
        <p id={id} className="flex items-center gap-1 pt-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-destructive">
          <CircleAlert aria-hidden="true" className="size-3 shrink-0" />
          {shown}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function AuthField({
  label,
  icon: Icon,
  error,
  valid,
  invalid,
  type = 'text',
  className,
  style,
  'aria-describedby': describedBy,
  ...props
}: AuthFieldProps) {
  const id = useId()
  const errorId = `${id}-error`
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false)
  const isPassword = type === 'password'
  const state = error || invalid ? 'error' : valid ? 'valid' : 'idle'

  return (
    <div className={cn('relative', className)} style={style}>
      <div className="relative">
        <input
          id={id}
          data-state={state}
          type={isPassword && revealed ? 'text' : type}
          placeholder=" "
          aria-invalid={state === 'error'}
          aria-describedby={cn(error && errorId, describedBy) || undefined}
          className={cn(
            'peer h-12 w-full border-0 border-b bg-transparent pt-4 text-sm text-foreground outline-none transition-[border-color] duration-300',
            isPassword ? 'pr-14' : 'pr-8',
            'border-foreground/20 hover:border-foreground/35 focus:border-foreground/10',
            'data-[state=error]:border-destructive/60 data-[state=valid]:border-secondary/50',
          )}
          {...props}
        />
        <label
          htmlFor={id}
          className={cn(
            'pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 left-0 origin-left -translate-y-1/2 text-sm text-muted-foreground transition-all duration-300',
            'peer-focus:top-2 peer-focus:text-[11px] peer-focus:text-primary',
            'peer-[:not(:placeholder-shown)]:top-2 peer-[:not(:placeholder-shown)]:text-[11px]',
            'peer-data-[state=error]:text-destructive',
          )}
        >
          {label}
        </label>
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-[1.5px] w-full origin-left scale-x-0 bg-primary transition-[scale,background-color] duration-500 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.65,0,0.35,1)] peer-focus:scale-x-100 peer-data-[state=error]:bg-destructive"
        />

        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={cn('absolute bottom-3 size-4', isPassword ? 'right-7' : 'right-0.5')}
        >
          {!isPassword && (
            <Icon
              className={cn(
                'absolute inset-0 size-4 text-muted-foreground transition-[opacity,scale] duration-300',
                state === 'valid' ? 'scale-50 opacity-0' : 'scale-100 opacity-100',
                state === 'error' && 'text-destructive/80',
              )}
            />
          )}
          <Check
            strokeWidth={2.5}
            className={cn(
              'absolute inset-0 size-4 text-secondary transition-[opacity,scale] duration-300',
              state === 'valid' ? 'scale-100 opacity-100' : 'scale-50 opacity-0',
            )}
          />
        </span>

        {isPassword && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setRevealed((v) => !v)}
            aria-label={revealed ? `Hide ${label.toLowerCase()}` : `Show ${label.toLowerCase()}`}
            aria-pressed={revealed}
            className="absolute right-0 bottom-2.5 rounded p-0.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-primary focus-visible:text-primary focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary"
          >
            {revealed ? <EyeOff className="size-4" /> : <Eye className="size-4" />}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <FieldMessage id={errorId} message={error} />
    </div>
  )
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\brand-panel.tsx ==========

import { Compass, Plane } from 'lucide-react'
import Image from 'next/image'
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import type { AuthMode } from './auth-card'

const JOURNEY = ['Plan', 'Travel', 'Explore', 'Return']

// Different backgrounds for GHOOMI authentication pages
function getBackgroundImage(
  mode: AuthMode,
  recovery: boolean
) {
  if (recovery) {
    return '/images/ghoomi-journey.png'
  }

  if (mode === 'signup') {
    return '/images/ghoomi-signup-beach.webp'
  }

  return '/images/ghoomi-traveller.png'
}

function Wordmark({
  className,
}: {
  className?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className={cn('flex items-center', className)}>
      <Image
        src="/brand/ghoomi-logo-white.png"
        alt="GHOOMI — Explore More"
        width={220}
        height={113}
        className="brand-logo object-contain object-left"
        priority
      />
    </div>
  )
}

function JourneyIndicator() {
  return (
    <ol
      aria-label="Your journey with Ghoomi"
      className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-medium tracking-[0.16em] text-white/85 uppercase sm:gap-2 sm:tracking-[0.22em]"
    >
      {JOURNEY.map((step, index) => (
        <li key={step} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className={cn(
                'size-1.5 rounded-full',
                index === 0
                  ? 'bg-accent'
                  : 'bg-white/45'
              )}
            />
            {step}
          </span>

          {index < JOURNEY.length - 1 && (
            <span className="h-px w-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-white/60 to-white/15 sm:w-5" />
          )}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  )
}

            ['--float-delay' as string]: '-3s',
          } as CSSProperties
        }

        <Compass className="size-4" />
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}

function BrandCopy({
  align,
  active,
}: {
  align: 'left' | 'right'
  active: boolean
}) {
  const dir = align === 'left' ? -1 : 1

  const item = (i: number): CSSProperties => ({
    ['--i' as string]: i,
    ['--dir' as string]: dir,
  })

  return (
    <div
      data-active={active}
      aria-hidden={!active}
      className={cn(
        'stagger-group absolute inset-y-0 flex w-[40%] flex-col p-12',
        align === 'left'
          ? 'left-0 items-start text-left'
          : 'right-0 items-end text-right'
      )}
    >
      <div className="stagger-item" style={item(0)}>
        <Wordmark />
      </div>

      <div
        className={cn(
          'mt-10 flex flex-col gap-4',
          align === 'right' && 'items-end'
        )}
      >
        <p
          className="stagger-item text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.3em] text-accent uppercase"
          style={item(1)}
        >
          {align === 'left'
            ? 'Welcome traveller'
            : 'New journey'}
        </p>

        <h2
          className="stagger-item font-display text-4xl leading-[1.05] font-bold text-balance text-white lg:text-5xl"
          style={item(2)}
        >
          Your journey starts here.
        </h2>

        <p
          className="stagger-item max-w-xs text-sm leading-relaxed text-pretty text-white/80"
          style={item(3)}
        >
          Plan, explore, book and manage your entire
          journey with Ghoomi.
        </p>

      >
        <RouteDecor align={align} />

    </div>
  )
}

}: {

  recovery?: boolean
}) {

    ? mode === 'signup'
      ? 'to-signup'
      : 'to-login'
    : undefined

    mode,
    recovery
  )

      <div
        data-mode={mode}
        data-animate={animation}
        style={{ ['--e' as string]: '3px' }}
        className="brand-clip brand-edge absolute inset-0 bg-primary"

        data-mode={mode}
        data-animate={animation}
        className="brand-clip absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden bg-ink"
      >

          <div
            data-mode={mode}
            className="brand-media absolute inset-y-0 left-[-25%] w-[150%]"
          >
            <Image
              src={backgroundImage}
              alt={
                recovery
                  ? 'Scenic mountain lake and winding roads'
                  : mode === 'signup'
                    ? 'Tropical beach with turquoise water and palm trees'
                    : 'Beautiful alpine mountain landscape and winding road'
              }
              fill
              priority
              sizes="1560px"
              className={cn(
                'object-cover',
                mode === 'signup' && !recovery
                  ? 'object-center'
                  : 'object-center'
              )}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Preserve existing dark overlays */}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-ink/75 via-ink/20 to-primary/25" />

          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-ink/70 via-transparent to-ink/30" />
        </div>
      </div>

      <BrandCopy
        align="left"
        active={mode === 'login'}
      />

      <BrandCopy
        align="right"
        active={mode === 'signup'}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

'use client'

import { BedDouble, Check, ShieldCheck, Ticket, UtensilsCrossed } from 'lucide-react'
import { useId, type CSSProperties } from 'react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { FieldMessage } from './auth-field'

export const CATEGORIES = [
  { id: 'hotels', title: 'Hotels', description: 'Stays, rooms & resorts', icon: BedDouble },
  { id: 'restaurants', title: 'Restaurants', description: 'Dining & local food', icon: UtensilsCrossed },
  { id: 'tours', title: 'Tours', description: 'Tours, passes & services', icon: Ticket },
] as const

export type CategoryId = (typeof CATEGORIES)[number]['id']

export function CategoryPicker({
  selected,
  onToggle,
  error,
  className,
}: {
  selected: CategoryId[]
  onToggle: (id: CategoryId) => void
  error?: string
  className?: string
}) {
  const id = useId()
  const titleId = `${id}-title`
  const descId = `${id}-desc`
  const errorId = `${id}-error`

  return (
    <div role="group" aria-labelledby={titleId} aria-describedby={cn(descId, error && errorId)} className={className}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <p id={titleId} className="text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">
          Business category
        </p>
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-secondary/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-secondary">
          <ShieldCheck aria-hidden="true" className="size-3" />
          Admin registration
        </span>
      </div>
      <p id={descId} className="mt-1 mb-3 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
        Choose one business category to continue.
      </p>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
        {CATEGORIES.map((category, i) => {
          const checked = selected.includes(category.id)
          const Icon = category.icon
          return (
            <label
              key={category.id}
              data-checked={checked}
              style={{ ['--i' as string]: i } as CSSProperties}
              className={cn(
                'category-card relative flex cursor-pointer flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border bg-card p-3 transition-[translate,scale,border-color,background-color,box-shadow] duration-300 ease-out',
                'hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-[0_10px_24px_-14px_rgb(23_32_51/0.35)] has-focus-visible:ring-2 has-focus-visible:ring-primary/30',
                checked
                  ? 'scale-[1.03] border-primary bg-primary/[0.04] shadow-[0_10px_24px_-14px_rgb(37_99_235/0.55)]'
                  : error
                    ? 'border-destructive/40'
                    : 'border-border hover:border-primary/40',
              )}
            >
              <input
                type="radio"
                name="businessCategory" 
                value={category.id}
                checked={checked}
                onChange={() => onToggle(category.id)}
                className="sr-only"
              />
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className={cn(
                  'absolute top-2 right-2 flex size-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full border transition-all duration-300',
                  checked ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground' : 'border-foreground/20',
                )}
              >
                <Check strokeWidth={3.5} className={cn('size-2.5 transition-[scale,opacity] duration-300', checked ? 'scale-100 opacity-100' : 'scale-50 opacity-0')} />
              </span>
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className={cn(
                  'flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-colors duration-300',
                  checked ? 'bg-primary text-primary-foreground' : 'bg-secondary/10 text-secondary',
                )}
              >
                <Icon className="size-4" />
              </span>
              <span className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-foreground">{category.title}</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground">{category.description}</span>
              </span>
            </label>
          )
        })}
      </div>
      <FieldMessage id={errorId} message={error} />
    </div>
  )
}

import { Check, CircleAlert, Loader2, Mail } from 'lucide-react'
import type { CSSProperties, ReactNode } from 'react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

export function staggerStyle(i: number, dir: 1 | -1): CSSProperties {
  return { ['--i' as string]: i, ['--dir' as string]: dir }
}


export function FormShell({
  active,
  className,
  children,
}: {
  active: boolean
  className?: string
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <div
      data-active={active}
      inert={!active}
      className={cn(
        'ghoomi-auth-form stagger-group flex min-h-0 flex-col justify-start overflow-visible px-6 py-6 sm:px-10 md:overflow-y-auto md:px-10 md:py-4 lg:px-12',
        className
      )}
    >
      <div className="my-auto mx-auto w-full max-w-sm">
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


export function Collapsible({ open, className, children }: { open: boolean; className?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className={cn('collapsible', className)} data-open={open} inert={!open} aria-hidden={!open}>
      <div className="-m-1 min-h-0 overflow-hidden p-1">{children}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function PrimaryButton({ children, loading = false }: { children: ReactNode; loading?: boolean }) {

    >
      {loading && <Loader2 aria-hidden="true" className="size-4 animate-spin" />}

    </button>
  )
}
