========== \frontend\src\components\auth\login-form.tsx ==========
'use client'
import { AtSign, Lock } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { AuthField } from './auth-field'
import { Checkbox, Divider, EmailButton, FormAlert, FormShell, FormStatus, GoogleButton, PrimaryButton, staggerStyle } from './form-parts'
import { useFormState } from './use-form-state'
import { emailError } from './validation'
import { authApi } from '@/services/auth-api'
const FIELDS = ['email', 'password'] as const
type Values = Record<(typeof FIELDS)[number], string>
function validate(values: Values) {
  const errors: Partial<Values> = {}
  errors.email = emailError(values.email)
  if (!values.password) errors.password = 'Password is required'
  return errors
}

export function LoginForm({ active, onSwitch, className }: { active: boolean; onSwitch: () => void; className?: string }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  const form = useFormState(FIELDS, validate, () => { setError(null); setStatus(null) })
  const s = (i: number) => staggerStyle(i, 1)
  const email = form.field('email')

  const handleSubmit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault()
    const element = event.currentTarget
    setStatus(null)
    if (!form.submit()) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => element.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[aria-invalid="true"]')?.focus())
      return
    }
    setPending(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      await authApi.login({ email: form.values.email.trim().toLowerCase(), password: form.values.password, rememberMe: Boolean(element.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('[name="remember"]')?.checked) })
      setStatus('Sign in confirmed by the authentication service. Your dashboard will be available after it is built.')
    } catch (e) { setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Unable to sign in.') }
    finally { setPending(false) }
  }
  const handleGoogle = async () => {
    setPending(true); setError(null)
    try { await authApi.startGoogleAuth() }
    catch (e) { setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Google authentication is unavailable.'); setPending(false) }
  }
  return (
    <FormShell active={active} className={className}>
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2 className="stagger-item font-display text-3xl font-bold text-foreground" style={s(0)}>Welcome back</h2>
        <p className="stagger-item text-sm text-muted-foreground" style={s(1)}>Continue your journey with Ghoomi.</p>
      </div>
      <form noValidate onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        <AuthField className="stagger-item" style={s(2)} {...email} invalid={Boolean(error)} type="email" label="Email Address" icon={AtSign} autoComplete="email" required />
        <div className="stagger-item flex flex-col" style={s(3)}>
          <AuthField {...form.field('password')} invalid={Boolean(error)} type="password" label="Password" icon={Lock} autoComplete="current-password" />
          <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <Checkbox name="remember" label="Remember me" />
            <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-primary">Forgot password?</Link>
          </div>
          <FormAlert message={error} />
        </div>
        <div className="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <div className="stagger-item" style={s(4)}><PrimaryButton loading={pending}>Login</PrimaryButton></div>
          <div className="stagger-item" style={s(5)}><Divider /></div>
          <div className="stagger-item" style={s(6)}><GoogleButton disabled={pending} onClick={handleGoogle} /></div>
          <div className="stagger-item" style={s(7)}><EmailButton onClick={() => document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('input[name="email"]')?.focus()} /></div>
        </div>
        <FormStatus message={status} />
      </form>
      <p className="stagger-item mt-2 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground" style={s(8)}>
        {"Don't have an account? "}
        <button type="button" onClick={onSwitch} className="font-semibold text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline focus-visible:underline">Create an account</button>
      </p>
    </FormShell>
  )
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\password-strength.tsx ==========

import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { PASSWORD_RULES } from './validation'

const LEVELS = {
  empty: {
    label: 'Not set',
    bars: 0,
    bar: 'bg-border',
    text: 'text-muted-foreground',
  },
  weak: {
    label: 'Weak',
    bars: 1,
    bar: 'bg-red-500',
    text: 'text-red-500',
  },
  fair: {
    label: 'Fair',
    bars: 2,
    bar: 'bg-amber-500',
    text: 'text-amber-500',
  },
  strong: {
    label: 'Strong',
    bars: 3,
    bar: 'bg-emerald-500',
    text: 'text-emerald-600',
  },
}

export function PasswordStrength({
  id,
  password,
  className,
}: {
  id: string
  password: string
  className?: string
}) {
  const met = PASSWORD_RULES.map(rule => rule.test(password))
  const score = met.filter(Boolean).length

  const level =
    LEVELS[
      score === 5
        ? 'strong'
        : score >= 3
          ? 'fair'
          : score > 0
            ? 'weak'
            : 'empty'
    ]

  // Display only one missing requirement at a time.
  // Prioritize password length and special characters.
  const missing = PASSWORD_RULES.filter(
    rule => !rule.test(password)
  )

  const missingRule =
    missing.find(rule => rule.id === 'length') ??
    missing.find(rule => rule.id === 'special') ??
    missing[0]

  return (
    <div id={id} className={className}>

      {/* Password Strength Indicator */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="grid flex-1 grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
          {[0, 1, 2].map(i => (
            <span
              key={i}
              className="h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-border"
            >
              <span
                className={cn(
                  'block h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300',
                  i < level.bars
                    ? cn(level.bar, 'w-full')
                    : 'w-0'
                )}
              />
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>

        <span
          className={cn(
            'w-14 text-right text-xs font-semibold',
            level.text
          )}
        >
          {level.label}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Dynamic Password Warning */}
      {password.length > 0 && (
        <div
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={cn(
            'mt-2 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed',
            missingRule
              ? 'text-amber-600'
              : 'text-emerald-600'
          )}
        >
          {missingRule ? (
            <>
              <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-3.5 shrink-0" />
              <span>
                {missingRule.message}
              </span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <CheckCircle2 className="size-3.5 shrink-0" />
              <span>
                Strong password! All requirements are met.
              </span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\recovery-controls.tsx ==========
'use client'
import { Eye, EyeOff, LoaderCircle } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState, type InputHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode } from 'react'
export function RecoveryInput({ label, type = 'text', ...props }: { label: string } & InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false)
  const password = type === 'password'
  return <label className="block"><span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">{label}</span>
    <span className="relative block"><input {...props} type={password && visible ? 'text' : type} aria-label={label} className="recovery-input pr-11 text-sm" />
    {password && <button type="button" aria-label={visible ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'} onClick={() => setVisible(!visible)} className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary">{visible ? <EyeOff className="size-4" /> : <Eye className="size-4" />}</button>}</span>
  </label>
}
export function RecoveryButton({ loading, children }: { loading: boolean; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-primary text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-60">
    {loading && <LoaderCircle aria-hidden="true" className="size-4 animate-spin" />}{children}
  </button>
}
export function RecoveryFeedback({ error, message }: { error?: string | null; message?: string | null }) {
  return <div aria-live="polite" className="space-y-2">{error && <p role="alert" className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-red-700">{error}</p>}{message && <p role="status" className="rounded-lg border border-teal-200 bg-teal-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-teal-800">{message}</p>}</div>
}

========== \frontend\src\components\auth\role-selector.tsx ==========
'use client'

import { Building2, UserRound } from 'lucide-react'
import { useRef, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

export type Role = 'user' | 'admin'

const OPTIONS: { value: Role; label: string; icon: typeof UserRound }[] = [
  { value: 'user', label: 'User', icon: UserRound },
  { value: 'admin', label: 'Admin', icon: Building2 },
]

export function RoleSelector({ value, onChange }: { value: Role; onChange: (role: Role) => void }) {
  const refs = useRef<(HTMLButtonElement | null)[]>([])
  const index = OPTIONS.findIndex((o) => o.value === value)

  const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown'].includes(event.key)) return
    event.preventDefault()
    const next = index === 0 ? 1 : 0
    onChange(OPTIONS[next].value)
    refs.current[next]?.focus()
  }

  return (
    <div
      role="radiogroup"
      aria-label="Account type"
      onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
      className="relative grid grid-cols-2 rounded-full border border-border bg-muted p-1"
    >
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        style={{ transform: `translate3d(${index * 100}%, 0, 0)` }}
        className="absolute inset-y-1 left-1 w-[calc(50%-0.25rem)] rounded-full bg-card shadow-[0_1px_3px_rgb(23_32_51/0.12)] ring-1 ring-border transition-transform duration-500 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.65,0,0.35,1)]"
      />
      {OPTIONS.map((option, i) => {
        const checked = option.value === value
        const Icon = option.icon
        return (
          <button
            key={option.value}
            ref={(el) => {
              refs.current[i] = el
            }}
            type="button"
            role="radio"
            aria-checked={checked}
            tabIndex={checked ? 0 : -1}
            onClick={() => onChange(option.value)}
            className={cn(
              'relative z-10 flex h-9 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors duration-300 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-1 focus-visible:outline-primary',
              checked ? 'text-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground',
            )}
          >
            <Icon aria-hidden="true" className={cn('size-3.5 transition-colors duration-300', checked && 'text-primary')} />
            {option.label}
          </button>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\signup-form.tsx ==========

'use client'

import { Building2, Lock, Mail, MapPin, Phone, ShieldCheck, User } from 'lucide-react'
import { useId, useState, type CSSProperties, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { AuthField } from './auth-field'
import { CATEGORIES, CategoryPicker, type CategoryId } from './category-picker'
import {
  Collapsible,
  Divider,
  EmailButton,
  FormAlert,
  FormShell,
  FormStatus,
  GoogleButton,
  PrimaryButton,
  staggerStyle,
} from './form-parts'
import { PasswordStrength } from './password-strength'
import { RoleSelector, type Role } from './role-selector'
import { useFormState } from './use-form-state'
import { emailError, passwordError } from './validation'
import { authApi } from '@/services/auth-api'

const FIELDS = ['name', 'email', 'phone', 'password', 'confirm'] as const
type Values = Record<(typeof FIELDS)[number], string>
type AdminStep = 'category' | 'account' | 'business'
type SlideDirection = 'forward' | 'back'

function validate(values: Values) {
  const errors: Partial<Values> = {}

  if (!values.name.trim()) errors.name = 'Name is required'

  errors.email = emailError(values.email)

  const phone = values.phone.trim()
  if (!phone) errors.phone = 'Phone number is required'
  else if (!/^[6-9]\d{9}$/.test(phone)) {
    errors.phone = 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number'
  }

  errors.password = passwordError(values.password)

  if (!values.confirm) errors.confirm = 'Confirm password is required'
  else if (values.confirm !== values.password) {
    errors.confirm = 'Passwords do not match'
  }

  return errors
}

function getBusinessFields(category: CategoryId) {
  if (category === 'hotels') {
    return { name: 'Hotel / Property Name', type: 'Property Type' }
  }

  if (category === 'restaurants') {
    return { name: 'Restaurant Name', type: 'Cuisine Type' }
  }

  return { name: 'Tour Company Name', type: 'Tour Service Type' }
}

export function SignupForm({
  active,
  onSwitch,
  className,
}: {
  active: boolean
  onSwitch: () => void
  className?: string
}) {
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role>('user')
  const [adminStep, setAdminStep] = useState<AdminStep>('category')
  const [slideDirection, setSlideDirection] = useState<SlideDirection>('forward')
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<CategoryId[]>([])

  const [businessName, setBusinessName] = useState('')
  const [businessType, setBusinessType] = useState('')
  const [businessCity, setBusinessCity] = useState('')

  const [passwordFocused, setPasswordFocused] = useState(false)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)

  const form = useFormState(FIELDS, validate, () => {
    setStatus(null)
    setError(null)
  })

  const strengthId = useId()
  const s = (i: number) => staggerStyle(i, -1)
  const isAdmin = role === 'admin'
  const selectedCategory = categories[0] ?? null
  const fields = selectedCategory ? getBusinessFields(selectedCategory) : null
  const categoryTitle = CATEGORIES.find(
    (category) => category.id === selectedCategory
  )?.title

  const animationClass =
    slideDirection === 'back' ? 'admin-step-back' : 'admin-step-forward'

  const swapDir = {
    ['--dir' as string]: isAdmin ? 1 : -1,
  } as CSSProperties

  const changeRole = (next: Role) => {
    if (next === role || pending) return

    setRole(next)
    setAdminStep('category')
    setSlideDirection('forward')
    setStatus(null)
    setError(null)
  }

  // Selecting a business category automatically opens account details.
  const chooseCategory = (id: CategoryId) => {
    setCategories([id])
    setBusinessName('')
    setBusinessType('')
    setBusinessCity('')
    setError(null)
    setSlideDirection('forward')
    setAdminStep('account')
  }

  const previousStep = (step: AdminStep) => {
    setError(null)
    setStatus(null)
    setSlideDirection('back')
    setAdminStep(step)
  }

  const continueToBusiness = () => {
    setError(null)
    setStatus(null)

    if (!form.submit()) return

    setSlideDirection('forward')
    setAdminStep('business')
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (pending) return

    setError(null)
    setStatus(null)

    if (isAdmin && (adminStep !== 'business' || !selectedCategory)) {
      setError('Please choose a business category before registering.')
      return
    }

    if (!form.submit()) {
      if (isAdmin) {
        setSlideDirection('back')
        setAdminStep('account')
      }
      return
    }

    if (
      isAdmin &&
      (!businessName.trim() || !businessType.trim() || !businessCity.trim())
    ) {
      setError('Please complete all business details.')
      return
    }

    setPending(true)

    try {
      // Business registration creates a partner application.
      // It does not grant privileged administrator permissions.
      await authApi.register({
        name: form.values.name.trim(),
        email: form.values.email.trim().toLowerCase(),
        phone: `+91${form.values.phone.trim()}`,
        password: form.values.password,
        accountType: isAdmin ? 'partner_application' : 'traveller',

        ...(isAdmin
          ? {
              businessCategories: categories,
              businessDetails: {
                businessName: businessName.trim(),
                businessType: businessType.trim(),
                city: businessCity.trim(),
              },
            }
          : {}),
      })

      setStatus(
        isAdmin
          ? 'Your business application was accepted by the server for review. No admin access has been granted.'
          : 'Your account registration was accepted by the server.'
      )
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Unable to create account.')
    } finally {
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  const handleGoogle = async () => {
    if (pending) return

    setPending(true)
    setError(null)

    try {
      await authApi.startGoogleAuth()
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        e instanceof Error
          ? e.message
          : 'Google authentication is unavailable.'
      )
      setPending(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <FormShell active={active} className={className}>
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2
          className="stagger-item font-display text-3xl font-bold text-foreground"
          style={s(0)}
        >
          Create your account
        </h2>

        <div className="stagger-item overflow-hidden" style={s(1)}>
          <p
            key={role}
            className="mode-swap text-sm text-muted-foreground"
            style={swapDir}
          >
            {isAdmin
              ? 'Request a business partnership and welcome travellers.'
              : 'Start planning better journeys with Ghoomi.'}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="stagger-item mb-3" style={s(2)}>
        <RoleSelector value={role} onChange={changeRole} />
      </div>

      {/* STEP 1 — BUSINESS CATEGORY */}
      {isAdmin && adminStep === 'category' && (
        <div className="stagger-item" style={s(3)}>
          <div key="category" className={`${animationClass} space-y-3`}>
            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-primary">
              Step 1 of 3 · Choose a business category
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Select Hotels, Restaurants, or Tours to continue.
            </p>
            <CategoryPicker
              selected={categories}
              onToggle={chooseCategory}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {(!isAdmin || adminStep !== 'category') && (
        <form
          noValidate
          onSubmit={handleSubmit}
          className="stagger-item flex flex-col gap-3"
          style={s(3)}
        >
          {/* USER SIGNUP / ADMIN STEP 2 */}
          {(!isAdmin || adminStep === 'account') && (
            <div
              key="account"
              className={`${isAdmin ? animationClass : ''} space-y-3`}
            >
              {isAdmin && (
                <p className="text-xs font-medium text-primary">
                  Step 2 of 3 · {categoryTitle} · Account details
                </p>
              )}

              <AuthField
                className="stagger-item"
                style={s(3)}
                {...form.field('name')}
                label="Name"
                icon={User}
                autoComplete="name"
              />

              <AuthField
                className="stagger-item"
                style={s(4)}
                {...form.field('email')}
  type="email"
  label="Email Address"
  icon={Mail}
  autoComplete="email"
  required
/>
<AuthField
  className="stagger-item"
  style={s(5)}
  {...form.field('phone')}
                type="tel"
                label="Phone Number"
                icon={Phone}
                autoComplete="tel"
              />

              <div className="stagger-item" style={s(5)}>
                <AuthField
                  {...form.field('password')}
                  onFocus={() => setPasswordFocused(true)}
                  onBlur={() => {
                    setPasswordFocused(false)
                    form.field('password').onBlur()
                  }}
                  aria-describedby={strengthId}
                  type="password"
                  label="Password"
                  icon={Lock}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                />

                <Collapsible
                  open={passwordFocused || form.values.password.length > 0}
                >
                  <PasswordStrength
                    id={strengthId}
                    password={form.values.password}
                    className="pt-2"
                  />
                </Collapsible>
              </div>

              <AuthField
                className="stagger-item"
                style={s(6)}
                {...form.field('confirm')}
                type="password"
                label="Confirm Password"
                icon={ShieldCheck}
                autoComplete="new-password"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ADMIN STEP 3 — BUSINESS DETAILS */}
          {isAdmin && adminStep === 'business' && selectedCategory && fields && (
            <div key="business" className={`${animationClass} space-y-3`}>
              <p className="text-xs font-medium text-primary">
                Step 3 of 3 · {categoryTitle} · Business details
              </p>

              <AuthField
                name="businessName"
                label={fields.name}
                icon={Building2}
                value={businessName}
                required
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setBusinessName(e.target.value)
                  setError(null)
                }}
              />

              <AuthField
                name="businessType"
                label={fields.type}
                icon={Building2}
                value={businessType}
                required
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setBusinessType(e.target.value)
                  setError(null)
                }}
              />

              <AuthField
                name="businessCity"
                label="City"
                icon={MapPin}
                value={businessCity}
                required
                autoComplete="address-level2"
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setBusinessCity(e.target.value)
                  setError(null)
                }}
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => previousStep('account')}
                className="text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
              >
                Back to Account Details
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* WIZARD NAVIGATION */}
          {isAdmin && adminStep === 'account' ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={continueToBusiness}
                className="h-11 w-full rounded-full bg-primary text-sm font-semibold text-primary-foreground transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
              >
                Continue to Business Details
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => previousStep('category')}
                className="text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline"
              >
                Back to Categories
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-3">
              <div className="stagger-item" style={s(7)}>
                <PrimaryButton loading={pending}>
                  Create Account
                </PrimaryButton>
              </div>

              <div className="stagger-item" style={s(8)}>
                <Divider />
              </div>

              <div className="stagger-item" style={s(9)}>
                <GoogleButton
                  disabled={pending}
                  onClick={handleGoogle}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="stagger-item" style={s(10)}>
                <EmailButton
                  onClick={() => {
                    setError('Email registration has not been configured yet.')
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <FormAlert message={error} />
          <FormStatus message={status} />
        </form>
      )}

      <p
        className="stagger-item mt-2 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground"
        style={s(11)}
      >
        {'Already have an account? '}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onSwitch}
          className="font-semibold text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline focus-visible:underline"
        >
          Login
        </button>
      </p>
    </FormShell>
  )
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\use-form-state.ts ==========
'use client'

import { useState, type ChangeEvent } from 'react'

type Errors<F extends string> = Partial<Record<F, string>>

/**
 * Errors are derived live from values but only surfaced once a field is touched
 * (on blur or on submit), so corrections clear the error as the user types.
 */
export function useFormState<F extends string>(
  fields: readonly F[],
  validate: (values: Record<F, string>) => Errors<F>,
  onEdit?: () => void,
) {
  const [values, setValues] = useState(() => Object.fromEntries(fields.map((f) => [f, ''])) as Record<F, string>)
  const [touched, setTouched] = useState<Partial<Record<F, boolean>>>({})

  const errors = validate(values)
  const isValid = Object.values(errors).every((message) => !message)

  const field = (name: F) => ({
    name,
    value: values[name],
    onChange: (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
      const next = event.target.value
      setValues((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: next }))
      onEdit?.()
    },
    onBlur: () => setTouched((prev) => (prev[name] ? prev : { ...prev, [name]: true })),
    error: touched[name] ? errors[name] : undefined,
    valid: Boolean(touched[name] && values[name] && !errors[name]),
  })

  const submit = () => {
    setTouched(Object.fromEntries(fields.map((f) => [f, true])) as Record<F, boolean>)
    return isValid
  }

  return { values, field, submit }
}


========== \frontend\src\components\auth\validation.ts ==========
export const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/

export type PasswordRule = {
  id: string
  label: string
  message: string
  test: (value: string) => boolean
}

export const PASSWORD_RULES: PasswordRule[] = [
  { id: 'length', label: '8+ characters', message: 'Password must contain at least 8 characters', test: (v) => v.length >= 8 },
  { id: 'upper', label: 'Uppercase letter', message: 'Password must include an uppercase letter', test: (v) => /[A-Z]/.test(v) },
  { id: 'lower', label: 'Lowercase letter', message: 'Password must include a lowercase letter', test: (v) => /[a-z]/.test(v) },
  { id: 'number', label: 'Number', message: 'Password must include a number', test: (v) => /\d/.test(v) },
  { id: 'special', label: 'Special character', message: 'Password must include a special character', test: (v) => /[^A-Za-z0-9\s]/.test(v) },
]

export function emailError(value: string) {
  const email = value.trim()
  if (!email) return 'Email is required'
  if (!EMAIL_PATTERN.test(email)) return 'Please enter a valid email'
  return undefined
}

export function phoneError(value: string) {
  const phone = value.trim()
  if (!phone) return 'Phone number is required'
  if (!/^[6-9]\d{9}$/.test(phone)) return 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number'
  return undefined
}
export function passwordError(value: string) {
  if (!value) return 'Password is required'
  return PASSWORD_RULES.find((rule) => !rule.test(value))?.message
}

export const wait = (ms: number) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))

========== \frontend\src\services\auth-api.ts ==========
/**
 * Frontend-only integration contract. These are expected backend routes, NOT implemented APIs.
 * The backend must provide HTTP-only secure session cookies, CSRF protection, server-side
 * role validation and OTP issuing/verification. Never store a token in localStorage.
 */
export type ApiSuccess = { success: true; message?: string }
export type RegistrationInput = {
  name: string
  email: string
  phone: string
  password: string
  accountType: 'traveller' | 'partner_application'

  businessCategories?: string[]

  businessDetails?: {
    businessName: string
    businessType: string
    city: string
  }
}
export type LoginInput = { email: string; password: string; rememberMe: boolean }

const apiBase = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_AUTH_API_BASE_URL?.trim().replace(/\/$/, '')
export class AuthApiError extends Error {}

async function send<T extends ApiSuccess>(path: string, payload: object): Promise<T> {
  if (!apiBase) throw new AuthApiError('Authentication is not configured yet. Connect a real backend first.')
  let response: Response
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${apiBase}${path}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      credentials: 'include',
      cache: 'no-store',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    })
  } catch {
    throw new AuthApiError('Authentication service cannot be reached. Please try again later.')
  }
  const result: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null)
  const body = result && typeof result === 'object' ? result as Record<string, unknown> : {}
  if (!response.ok || body.success !== true) {
    const message = typeof body.message === 'string' && body.message.length < 250 ? body.message : 'The request could not be completed.'
    throw new AuthApiError(message)
  }
  return body as T
}

export const authApi = {
  login: (input: LoginInput) => send<ApiSuccess>('/auth/login', input),
  register: (input: RegistrationInput) => send<ApiSuccess>('/auth/register', input),
  forgotPassword: (email: string) => send<ApiSuccess & { retryAfterSeconds?: number }>('/auth/password/forgot', { email }),
  verifyOtp: (email: string, otp: string) => send<ApiSuccess>('/auth/password/verify-otp', { email, otp }),
  /** Requires a short-lived server-issued reset grant in an HttpOnly cookie. */
  resetPassword: (newPassword: string) => send<ApiSuccess>('/auth/password/reset', { newPassword }),
  async startGoogleAuth(): Promise<void> {
    const response = await send<ApiSuccess & { authorizationUrl?: string }>('/auth/oauth/google/start', { intent: 'sign_in' })
    if (!response.authorizationUrl) throw new AuthApiError('Google authentication is not configured.')
    let url: URL
    try { url = new URL(response.authorizationUrl) }
    catch { throw new AuthApiError('Authentication provider returned an invalid URL.') }
    if (url.protocol !== 'https:' && !(url.protocol === 'http:' && url.hostname === 'localhost')) {
      throw new AuthApiError('Authentication provider returned an unsafe URL.')
    }
    window.location.assign(url.toString())
  },
}
